+// A sub-warp cooperatively evaluates one target body. Source positions are
+// staged once per block, so global reads are coalesced and shared by all
+// targets in that block.
+// The destination position arrays form the step barrier: a kernel only reads
+// the immutable current arrays and only writes the next arrays.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS_PER_BLOCK, 4)
+void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positionX,
+                   const double* __restrict__ positionY,
+                   const double* __restrict__ positionZ,
+                   double* __restrict__ nextPositionX,
+                   double* __restrict__ nextPositionY,
+                   double* __restrict__ nextPositionZ,
+                   double* __restrict__ velocityX,
+                   double* __restrict__ velocityY,
+                   double* __restrict__ velocityZ,
+                   int bodyCount) {
+    __shared__ double tileX[THREADS_PER_BLOCK];
+    __shared__ double tileY[THREADS_PER_BLOCK];
+    __shared__ double tileZ[THREADS_PER_BLOCK];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_TARGET - 1);
+    const int targetInBlock = threadIdx.x / THREADS_PER_TARGET;
+    const int target = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * TARGETS_PER_BLOCK + targetInBlock;
+    const bool active = target < bodyCount;
+
+    const double targetX = active ? positionX[target] : 0.0;
+    const double targetY = active ? positionY[target] : 0.0;
+    const double targetZ = active ? positionZ[target] : 0.0;
+    double forceX = 0.0;
+    double forceY = 0.0;
+    double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+    for (int tileBase = 0; tileBase < bodyCount; tileBase += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {
+        const int source = tileBase + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+        if (source < bodyCount) {
+            tileX[threadIdx.x] = positionX[source];
+            tileY[threadIdx.x] = positionY[source];
+            tileZ[threadIdx.x] = positionZ[source];
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        if (active) {
+            const int tileSize = min(THREADS_PER_BLOCK, bodyCount - tileBase);
+            for (int index = lane; index < tileSize; index += THREADS_PER_TARGET) {
+                const double dx = tileX[index] - targetX;
+                const double dy = tileY[index] - targetY;
+                const double dz = tileZ[index] - targetZ;
+                const double distanceSquared = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double inverseDistance = rsqrt(distanceSquared);
+                const double inverseDistanceCubed =
+                    inverseDistance * inverseDistance * inverseDistance;
+
+                forceX += dx * inverseDistanceCubed;
+                forceY += dy * inverseDistanceCubed;
+                forceZ += dz * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = THREADS_PER_TARGET / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {
+        forceX += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, forceX, offset, THREADS_PER_TARGET);
+        forceY += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, forceY, offset, THREADS_PER_TARGET);
+        forceZ += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, forceZ, offset, THREADS_PER_TARGET);
+    }
+
+    if (lane == 0 && active) {
+        const double newVelocityX = velocityX[target] + DT * forceX;
+        const double newVelocityY = velocityY[target] + DT * forceY;
+        const double newVelocityZ = velocityZ[target] + DT * forceZ;
+
+        velocityX[target] = newVelocityX;
+        velocityY[target] = newVelocityY;
+        velocityZ[target] = newVelocityZ;
+        nextPositionX[target] = targetX + newVelocityX * DT;
+        nextPositionY[target] = targetY + newVelocityY * DT;
+        nextPositionZ[target] = targetZ + newVelocityZ * DT;
     }
 }

+class DeviceSimulation {
+  public:
+    explicit DeviceSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies)
+        : bodyCount_(static_cast<int>(bodies.size())) {
+        if (bodyCount_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const size_t count = bodies.size();
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&state_), 9 * count * sizeof(double)));
+        for (int component = 0; component < 6; ++component) {
+            hostState_.emplace_back(count);
+        }
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+            hostState_[0][i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            hostState_[1][i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            hostState_[2][i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            hostState_[3][i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            hostState_[4][i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            hostState_[5][i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+
+        for (int component = 0; component < 3; ++component) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(position(0, component), hostState_[component].data(),
+                                  count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(velocity(component), hostState_[component + 3].data(),
+                                  count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        }
+
+        // Host staging is only needed again when results are requested.
+        hostState_.clear();
+        hostState_.shrink_to_fit();
+    }
+
+    DeviceSimulation(const DeviceSimulation&) = delete;
+    DeviceSimulation& operator=(const DeviceSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~DeviceSimulation() {
+        if (state_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(state_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    float run(int stepCount) {
+        cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
+        cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start));
+
+        if (bodyCount_ > 0) {
+            const int blockCount = (bodyCount_ + TARGETS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / TARGETS_PER_BLOCK;
+            for (int step = 0; step < stepCount; ++step) {
+                advanceBodies<<<blockCount, THREADS_PER_BLOCK>>>(
+                    position(activePosition_, 0), position(activePosition_, 1),
+                    position(activePosition_, 2), position(activePosition_ ^ 1, 0),
+                    position(activePosition_ ^ 1, 1), position(activePosition_ ^ 1, 2),
+                    velocity(0), velocity(1), velocity(2), bodyCount_);
+                activePosition_ ^= 1;
+            }
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop));
+        float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start, stop));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(start));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stop));
+        return elapsedMilliseconds;
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
+        if (bodyCount_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const size_t count = bodies.size();
+        hostState_.resize(6);
+        for (auto& component : hostState_) {
+            component.resize(count);
+        }
+        for (int component = 0; component < 3; ++component) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostState_[component].data(),
+                                  position(activePosition_, component), count * sizeof(double),
+                                  cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostState_[component + 3].data(), velocity(component),
+                                  count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        }
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(hostState_[0][i], hostState_[1][i], hostState_[2][i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(hostState_[3][i], hostState_[4][i], hostState_[5][i]);
+        }
     }
-}
 
+  private:
+    double* position(int buffer, int component) const {
+        return state_ + static_cast<size_t>(buffer * 3 + component) * bodyCount_;
+    }
+
+    double* velocity(int component) const {
+        return state_ + static_cast<size_t>(6 + component) * bodyCount_;
+    }
+
+    int bodyCount_ = 0;
+    int activePosition_ = 0;
+    double* state_ = nullptr;
+    std::vector<std::vector<double>> hostState_;

+// A sub-warp cooperatively evaluates one target body. Source positions are
+// staged once per block, so global reads are coalesced and shared by all
+// targets in that block.
+// The destination position arrays form the step barrier: a kernel only reads
+// the immutable current arrays and only writes the next arrays.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS_PER_BLOCK, 4)
+void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positionX,
+                   const double* __restrict__ positionY,
+                   const double* __restrict__ positionZ,
+                   double* __restrict__ nextPositionX,
+                   double* __restrict__ nextPositionY,
+                   double* __restrict__ nextPositionZ,
+                   double* __restrict__ velocityX,
+                   double* __restrict__ velocityY,
+                   double* __restrict__ velocityZ,
+                   int bodyCount) {
+    __shared__ double tileX[THREADS_PER_BLOCK];
+    __shared__ double tileY[THREADS_PER_BLOCK];
+    __shared__ double tileZ[THREADS_PER_BLOCK];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_TARGET - 1);
+    const int targetInBlock = threadIdx.x / THREADS_PER_TARGET;
+    const int target = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * TARGETS_PER_BLOCK + targetInBlock;
+    const bool active = target < bodyCount;
+
+    const double targetX = active ? positionX[target] : 0.0;
+    const double targetY = active ? positionY[target] : 0.0;
+    const double targetZ = active ? positionZ[target] : 0.0;
+    double forceX = 0.0;
+    double forceY = 0.0;
+    double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+    for (int tileBase = 0; tileBase < bodyCount; tileBase += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {
+        const int source = tileBase + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+        if (source < bodyCount) {
+            tileX[threadIdx.x] = positionX[source];
+            tileY[threadIdx.x] = positionY[source];
+            tileZ[threadIdx.x] = positionZ[source];
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        if (active) {
+            const int tileSize = min(THREADS_PER_BLOCK, bodyCount - tileBase);
+            for (int index = lane; index < tileSize; index += THREADS_PER_TARGET) {
+                const double dx = tileX[index] - targetX;
+                const double dy = tileY[index] - targetY;
+                const double dz = tileZ[index] - targetZ;
+                const double distanceSquared = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double inverseDistance = rsqrt(distanceSquared);
+                const double inverseDistanceCubed =
+                    inverseDistance * inverseDistance * inverseDistance;
+
+                forceX += dx * inverseDistanceCubed;
+                forceY += dy * inverseDistanceCubed;
+                forceZ += dz * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = THREADS_PER_TARGET / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {
+        forceX += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, forceX, offset, THREADS_PER_TARGET);
+        forceY += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, forceY, offset, THREADS_PER_TARGET);
+        forceZ += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, forceZ, offset, THREADS_PER_TARGET);
+    }
+
+    if (lane == 0 && active) {
+        const double newVelocityX = velocityX[target] + DT * forceX;
+        const double newVelocityY = velocityY[target] + DT * forceY;
+        const double newVelocityZ = velocityZ[target] + DT * forceZ;
+
+        velocityX[target] = newVelocityX;
+        velocityY[target] = newVelocityY;
+        velocityZ[target] = newVelocityZ;
+        nextPositionX[target] = targetX + newVelocityX * DT;
+        nextPositionY[target] = targetY + newVelocityY * DT;
+        nextPositionZ[target] = targetZ + newVelocityZ * DT;
     }
 }
 
-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {

+class DeviceSimulation {
+  public:
+    explicit DeviceSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies)
+        : bodyCount_(static_cast<int>(bodies.size())) {
+        if (bodyCount_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const size_t count = bodies.size();
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&state_), 9 * count * sizeof(double)));
+        for (int component = 0; component < 6; ++component) {
+            hostState_.emplace_back(count);
+        }
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+            hostState_[0][i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            hostState_[1][i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            hostState_[2][i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            hostState_[3][i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            hostState_[4][i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            hostState_[5][i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+
+        for (int component = 0; component < 3; ++component) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(position(0, component), hostState_[component].data(),
+                                  count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(velocity(component), hostState_[component + 3].data(),
+                                  count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        }
+
+        // Host staging is only needed again when results are requested.
+        hostState_.clear();
+        hostState_.shrink_to_fit();
+    }
+
+    DeviceSimulation(const DeviceSimulation&) = delete;
+    DeviceSimulation& operator=(const DeviceSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~DeviceSimulation() {
+        if (state_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(state_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    float run(int stepCount) {
+        cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
+        cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start));
+
+        if (bodyCount_ > 0) {
+            const int blockCount = (bodyCount_ + TARGETS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / TARGETS_PER_BLOCK;
+            for (int step = 0; step < stepCount; ++step) {
+                advanceBodies<<<blockCount, THREADS_PER_BLOCK>>>(
+                    position(activePosition_, 0), position(activePosition_, 1),
+                    position(activePosition_, 2), position(activePosition_ ^ 1, 0),
+                    position(activePosition_ ^ 1, 1), position(activePosition_ ^ 1, 2),
+                    velocity(0), velocity(1), velocity(2), bodyCount_);
+                activePosition_ ^= 1;
+            }
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop));
+        float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start, stop));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(start));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stop));
+        return elapsedMilliseconds;
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
+        if (bodyCount_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const size_t count = bodies.size();
+        hostState_.resize(6);
+        for (auto& component : hostState_) {
+            component.resize(count);
+        }
+        for (int component = 0; component < 3; ++component) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostState_[component].data(),
+                                  position(activePosition_, component), count * sizeof(double),
+                                  cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostState_[component + 3].data(), velocity(component),
+                                  count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        }
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(hostState_[0][i], hostState_[1][i], hostState_[2][i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(hostState_[3][i], hostState_[4][i], hostState_[5][i]);
+        }
     }
-}
 
+  private:
+    double* position(int buffer, int component) const {
+        return state_ + static_cast<size_t>(buffer * 3 + component) * bodyCount_;
+    }
+
+    double* velocity(int component) const {
+        return state_ + static_cast<size_t>(6 + component) * bodyCount_;
+    }
+
+    int bodyCount_ = 0;
+    int activePosition_ = 0;
+    double* state_ = nullptr;
+    std::vector<std::vector<double>> hostState_;
+};
+

+// A sub-warp cooperatively evaluates one target body. Source positions are
+// staged once per block, so global reads are coalesced and shared by all
+// targets in that block.
+// The destination position arrays form the step barrier: a kernel only reads
+// the immutable current arrays and only writes the next arrays.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS_PER_BLOCK, 4)
+void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positionX,
+                   const double* __restrict__ positionY,
+                   const double* __restrict__ positionZ,
+                   double* __restrict__ nextPositionX,
+                   double* __restrict__ nextPositionY,
+                   double* __restrict__ nextPositionZ,
+                   double* __restrict__ velocityX,
+                   double* __restrict__ velocityY,
+                   double* __restrict__ velocityZ,
+                   int bodyCount) {
+    __shared__ double tileX[THREADS_PER_BLOCK];
+    __shared__ double tileY[THREADS_PER_BLOCK];
+    __shared__ double tileZ[THREADS_PER_BLOCK];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_TARGET - 1);
+    const int targetInBlock = threadIdx.x / THREADS_PER_TARGET;
+    const int target = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * TARGETS_PER_BLOCK + targetInBlock;
+    const bool active = target < bodyCount;
+
+    const double targetX = active ? positionX[target] : 0.0;
+    const double targetY = active ? positionY[target] : 0.0;
+    const double targetZ = active ? positionZ[target] : 0.0;
+    double forceX = 0.0;
+    double forceY = 0.0;
+    double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+    for (int tileBase = 0; tileBase < bodyCount; tileBase += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {
+        const int source = tileBase + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+        if (source < bodyCount) {
+            tileX[threadIdx.x] = positionX[source];
+            tileY[threadIdx.x] = positionY[source];
+            tileZ[threadIdx.x] = positionZ[source];
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        if (active) {
+            const int tileSize = min(THREADS_PER_BLOCK, bodyCount - tileBase);
+            for (int index = lane; index < tileSize; index += THREADS_PER_TARGET) {
+                const double dx = tileX[index] - targetX;
+                const double dy = tileY[index] - targetY;
+                const double dz = tileZ[index] - targetZ;
+                const double distanceSquared = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double inverseDistance = rsqrt(distanceSquared);
+                const double inverseDistanceCubed =
+                    inverseDistance * inverseDistance * inverseDistance;
+
+                forceX += dx * inverseDistanceCubed;
+                forceY += dy * inverseDistanceCubed;
+                forceZ += dz * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = THREADS_PER_TARGET / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {
+        forceX += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, forceX, offset, THREADS_PER_TARGET);
+        forceY += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, forceY, offset, THREADS_PER_TARGET);
+        forceZ += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, forceZ, offset, THREADS_PER_TARGET);
+    }
+
+    if (lane == 0 && active) {
+        const double newVelocityX = velocityX[target] + DT * forceX;
+        const double newVelocityY = velocityY[target] + DT * forceY;
+        const double newVelocityZ = velocityZ[target] + DT * forceZ;
+
+        velocityX[target] = newVelocityX;
+        velocityY[target] = newVelocityY;
+        velocityZ[target] = newVelocityZ;
+        nextPositionX[target] = targetX + newVelocityX * DT;
+        nextPositionY[target] = targetY + newVelocityY * DT;
+        nextPositionZ[target] = targetZ + newVelocityZ * DT;
     }
 }

+class DeviceSimulation {
+  public:
+    explicit DeviceSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies)
+        : bodyCount_(static_cast<int>(bodies.size())) {
+        if (bodyCount_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const size_t count = bodies.size();
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&state_), 9 * count * sizeof(double)));
+        for (int component = 0; component < 6; ++component) {
+            hostState_.emplace_back(count);
+        }
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+            hostState_[0][i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            hostState_[1][i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            hostState_[2][i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            hostState_[3][i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            hostState_[4][i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            hostState_[5][i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+
+        for (int component = 0; component < 3; ++component) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(position(0, component), hostState_[component].data(),
+                                  count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(velocity(component), hostState_[component + 3].data(),
+                                  count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        }
+
+        // Host staging is only needed again when results are requested.
+        hostState_.clear();
+        hostState_.shrink_to_fit();
+    }
+
+    DeviceSimulation(const DeviceSimulation&) = delete;
+    DeviceSimulation& operator=(const DeviceSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~DeviceSimulation() {
+        if (state_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(state_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    float run(int stepCount) {
+        cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
+        cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start));
+
+        if (bodyCount_ > 0) {
+            const int blockCount = (bodyCount_ + TARGETS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / TARGETS_PER_BLOCK;
+            for (int step = 0; step < stepCount; ++step) {
+                advanceBodies<<<blockCount, THREADS_PER_BLOCK>>>(
+                    position(activePosition_, 0), position(activePosition_, 1),
+                    position(activePosition_, 2), position(activePosition_ ^ 1, 0),
+                    position(activePosition_ ^ 1, 1), position(activePosition_ ^ 1, 2),
+                    velocity(0), velocity(1), velocity(2), bodyCount_);
+                activePosition_ ^= 1;
+            }
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop));
+        float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start, stop));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(start));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stop));
+        return elapsedMilliseconds;
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
+        if (bodyCount_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const size_t count = bodies.size();
+        hostState_.resize(6);
+        for (auto& component : hostState_) {
+            component.resize(count);
+        }
+        for (int component = 0; component < 3; ++component) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostState_[component].data(),
+                                  position(activePosition_, component), count * sizeof(double),
+                                  cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostState_[component + 3].data(), velocity(component),
+                                  count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        }
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos = Vec3(hostState_[0][i], hostState_[1][i], hostState_[2][i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(hostState_[3][i], hostState_[4][i], hostState_[5][i]);
+        }
     }
-}
 
+  private:
+    double* position(int buffer, int component) const {
+        return state_ + static_cast<size_t>(buffer * 3 + component) * bodyCount_;
+    }
+
+    double* velocity(int component) const {
+        return state_ + static_cast<size_t>(6 + component) * bodyCount_;
+    }
+
+    int bodyCount_ = 0;
+    int activePosition_ = 0;
+    double* state_ = nullptr;
+    std::vector<std::vector<double>> hostState_;
+};

+// A sub-warp cooperatively evaluates one target body. Source positions are
+// staged once per block, so global reads are coalesced and shared by all
+// targets in that block.
+// The destination position arrays form the step barrier: a kernel only reads
+// the immutable current arrays and only writes the next arrays.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS_PER_BLOCK, 4)
+void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positionX,
+                   const double* __restrict__ positionY,
+                   const double* __restrict__ positionZ,
+                   double* __restrict__ nextPositionX,
+                   double* __restrict__ nextPositionY,
+                   double* __restrict__ nextPositionZ,
+                   double* __restrict__ velocityX,
+                   double* __restrict__ velocityY,
+                   double* __restrict__ velocityZ,
+                   int bodyCount) {
+    __shared__ double tileX[THREADS_PER_BLOCK];
+    __shared__ double tileY[THREADS_PER_BLOCK];
+    __shared__ double tileZ[THREADS_PER_BLOCK];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_TARGET - 1);
+    const int targetInBlock = threadIdx.x / THREADS_PER_TARGET;
+    const int target = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * TARGETS_PER_BLOCK + targetInBlock;
+    const bool active = target < bodyCount;
+
+    const double targetX = active ? positionX[target] : 0.0;
+    const double targetY = active ? positionY[target] : 0.0;
+    const double targetZ = active ? positionZ[target] : 0.0;
+    double forceX = 0.0;
+    double forceY = 0.0;
+    double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+    for (int tileBase = 0; tileBase < bodyCount; tileBase += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {
+        const int source = tileBase + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+        if (source < bodyCount) {
+            tileX[threadIdx.x] = positionX[source];
+            tileY[threadIdx.x] = positionY[source];
+            tileZ[threadIdx.x] = positionZ[source];
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        if (active) {
+            const int tileSize = min(THREADS_PER_BLOCK, bodyCount - tileBase);
+            for (int index = lane; index < tileSize; index += THREADS_PER_TARGET) {
+                const double dx = tileX[index] - targetX;
+                const double dy = tileY[index] - targetY;
+                const double dz = tileZ[index] - targetZ;
+                const double distanceSquared = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double inverseDistance = rsqrt(distanceSquared);
+                const double inverseDistanceCubed =
+                    inverseDistance * inverseDistance * inverseDistance;
+
+                forceX += dx * inverseDistanceCubed;
+                forceY += dy * inverseDistanceCubed;
+                forceZ += dz * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = THREADS_PER_TARGET / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {
+        forceX += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, forceX, offset, THREADS_PER_TARGET);
+        forceY += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, forceY, offset, THREADS_PER_TARGET);
+        forceZ += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, forceZ, offset, THREADS_PER_TARGET);
+    }
+
+    if (lane == 0 && active) {
+        const double newVelocityX = velocityX[target] + DT * forceX;
+        const double newVelocityY = velocityY[target] + DT * forceY;
+        const double newVelocityZ = velocityZ[target] + DT * forceZ;
+
+        velocityX[target] = newVelocityX;
+        velocityY[target] = newVelocityY;
+        velocityZ[target] = newVelocityZ;
+        nextPositionX[target] = targetX + newVelocityX * DT;
+        nextPositionY[target] = targetY + newVelocityY * DT;
+        nextPositionZ[target] = targetZ + newVelocityZ * DT;
     }
 }
 
-void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
-    for (auto& body : bodies) {
-        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
-        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
-        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+class DeviceSimulation {
+  public:
+    explicit DeviceSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies)
+        : bodyCount_(static_cast<int>(bodies.size())) {
+        if (bodyCount_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const size_t count = bodies.size();
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&state_), 9 * count * sizeof(double)));
+        for (int component = 0; component < 6; ++component) {
+            hostState_.emplace_back(count);
+        }
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+            hostState_[0][i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            hostState_[1][i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            hostState_[2][i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            hostState_[3][i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            hostState_[4][i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            hostState_[5][i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+
+        for (int component = 0; component < 3; ++component) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(position(0, component), hostState_[component].data(),
+                                  count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(velocity(component), hostState_[component + 3].data(),
+                                  count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        }
+
+        // Host staging is only needed again when results are requested.
+        hostState_.clear();
+        hostState_.shrink_to_fit();
+    }
+
+    DeviceSimulation(const DeviceSimulation&) = delete;
+    DeviceSimulation& operator=(const DeviceSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~DeviceSimulation() {
+        if (state_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(state_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    float run(int stepCount) {
+        cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
+        cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start));
+
+        if (bodyCount_ > 0) {
+            const int blockCount = (bodyCount_ + TARGETS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / TARGETS_PER_BLOCK;
+            for (int step = 0; step < stepCount; ++step) {
+                advanceBodies<<<blockCount, THREADS_PER_BLOCK>>>(
+                    position(activePosition_, 0), position(activePosition_, 1),
+                    position(activePosition_, 2), position(activePosition_ ^ 1, 0),
+                    position(activePosition_ ^ 1, 1), position(activePosition_ ^ 1, 2),
+                    velocity(0), velocity(1), velocity(2), bodyCount_);
+                activePosition_ ^= 1;
+            }
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop));
+        float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start, stop));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(start));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stop));
+        return elapsedMilliseconds;
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
+        if (bodyCount_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const size_t count = bodies.size();
+        hostState_.resize(6);
+        for (auto& component : hostState_) {
+            component.resize(count);
+        }
+        for (int component = 0; component < 3; ++component) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostState_[component].data(),

codex
The tuning result favors a full warp per body: it is essentially tied at 1,024 bodies and 10–17% faster on the larger sustained cases than the half-warp variant. I’ve kept that configuration. I’m also collapsing host staging into contiguous buffers so upload/download uses two bulk transfers instead of six small transfers, then I’ll do the final clean build and regression run.

+// A sub-warp cooperatively evaluates one target body. Source positions are
+// staged once per block, so global reads are coalesced and shared by all
+// targets in that block.
+// The destination position arrays form the step barrier: a kernel only reads
+// the immutable current arrays and only writes the next arrays.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS_PER_BLOCK, 4)
+void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positionX,
+                   const double* __restrict__ positionY,
+                   const double* __restrict__ positionZ,
+                   double* __restrict__ nextPositionX,
+                   double* __restrict__ nextPositionY,
+                   double* __restrict__ nextPositionZ,
+                   double* __restrict__ velocityX,
+                   double* __restrict__ velocityY,
+                   double* __restrict__ velocityZ,
+                   int bodyCount) {
+    __shared__ double tileX[THREADS_PER_BLOCK];
+    __shared__ double tileY[THREADS_PER_BLOCK];
+    __shared__ double tileZ[THREADS_PER_BLOCK];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_TARGET - 1);
+    const int targetInBlock = threadIdx.x / THREADS_PER_TARGET;
+    const int target = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * TARGETS_PER_BLOCK + targetInBlock;
+    const bool active = target < bodyCount;
+
+    const double targetX = active ? positionX[target] : 0.0;
+    const double targetY = active ? positionY[target] : 0.0;
+    const double targetZ = active ? positionZ[target] : 0.0;
+    double forceX = 0.0;
+    double forceY = 0.0;
+    double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+    for (int tileBase = 0; tileBase < bodyCount; tileBase += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {
+        const int source = tileBase + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+        if (source < bodyCount) {
+            tileX[threadIdx.x] = positionX[source];
+            tileY[threadIdx.x] = positionY[source];
+            tileZ[threadIdx.x] = positionZ[source];
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        if (active) {
+            const int tileSize = min(THREADS_PER_BLOCK, bodyCount - tileBase);
+            for (int index = lane; index < tileSize; index += THREADS_PER_TARGET) {
+                const double dx = tileX[index] - targetX;
+                const double dy = tileY[index] - targetY;
+                const double dz = tileZ[index] - targetZ;
+                const double distanceSquared = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double inverseDistance = rsqrt(distanceSquared);
+                const double inverseDistanceCubed =
+                    inverseDistance * inverseDistance * inverseDistance;
+
+                forceX += dx * inverseDistanceCubed;
+                forceY += dy * inverseDistanceCubed;
+                forceZ += dz * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = THREADS_PER_TARGET / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {
+        forceX += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, forceX, offset, THREADS_PER_TARGET);
+        forceY += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, forceY, offset, THREADS_PER_TARGET);
+        forceZ += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, forceZ, offset, THREADS_PER_TARGET);
+    }
+
+    if (lane == 0 && active) {
+        const double newVelocityX = velocityX[target] + DT * forceX;
+        const double newVelocityY = velocityY[target] + DT * forceY;
+        const double newVelocityZ = velocityZ[target] + DT * forceZ;
+
+        velocityX[target] = newVelocityX;
+        velocityY[target] = newVelocityY;
+        velocityZ[target] = newVelocityZ;
+        nextPositionX[target] = targetX + newVelocityX * DT;
+        nextPositionY[target] = targetY + newVelocityY * DT;
+        nextPositionZ[target] = targetZ + newVelocityZ * DT;
+    }

+class DeviceSimulation {
+  public:
+    explicit DeviceSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies)
+        : bodyCount_(static_cast<int>(bodies.size())) {
+        if (bodyCount_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const size_t count = bodies.size();
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&state_), 9 * count * sizeof(double)));
+        hostState_.resize(6 * count);
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+            hostState_[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            hostState_[count + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            hostState_[2 * count + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            hostState_[3 * count + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            hostState_[4 * count + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            hostState_[5 * count + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(position(0, 0), hostState_.data(), 3 * count * sizeof(double),
+                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(velocity(0), hostState_.data() + 3 * count,
+                              3 * count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+
+        // Host staging is only needed again when results are requested.
+        hostState_.clear();
+        hostState_.shrink_to_fit();
+    }
+
+    DeviceSimulation(const DeviceSimulation&) = delete;
+    DeviceSimulation& operator=(const DeviceSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~DeviceSimulation() {
+        if (state_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(state_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    float run(int stepCount) {
+        cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
+        cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start));
+
+        if (bodyCount_ > 0) {
+            const int blockCount = (bodyCount_ + TARGETS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / TARGETS_PER_BLOCK;
+            for (int step = 0; step < stepCount; ++step) {
+                advanceBodies<<<blockCount, THREADS_PER_BLOCK>>>(
+                    position(activePosition_, 0), position(activePosition_, 1),
+                    position(activePosition_, 2), position(activePosition_ ^ 1, 0),
+                    position(activePosition_ ^ 1, 1), position(activePosition_ ^ 1, 2),
+                    velocity(0), velocity(1), velocity(2), bodyCount_);
+                activePosition_ ^= 1;
+            }
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop));
+        float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start, stop));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(start));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stop));
+        return elapsedMilliseconds;
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
+        if (bodyCount_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const size_t count = bodies.size();
+        hostState_.resize(6 * count);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostState_.data(), position(activePosition_, 0),
+                              3 * count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostState_.data() + 3 * count, velocity(0),
+                              3 * count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos =
+                Vec3(hostState_[i], hostState_[count + i], hostState_[2 * count + i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(hostState_[3 * count + i], hostState_[4 * count + i],
+                                 hostState_[5 * count + i]);
+        }
+    }
+
+  private:
+    double* position(int buffer, int component) const {
+        return state_ + static_cast<size_t>(buffer * 3 + component) * bodyCount_;
+    }
+
+    double* velocity(int component) const {
+        return state_ + static_cast<size_t>(6 + component) * bodyCount_;
     }
-}
 
+    int bodyCount_ = 0;
+    int activePosition_ = 0;
+    double* state_ = nullptr;
+    std::vector<double> hostState_;
+};

     double energy = 0.0;
-    const size_t n = bodies.size();
-    
-    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)

+// A sub-warp cooperatively evaluates one target body. Source positions are
+// staged once per block, so global reads are coalesced and shared by all
+// targets in that block.
+// The destination position arrays form the step barrier: a kernel only reads
+// the immutable current arrays and only writes the next arrays.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS_PER_BLOCK, 4)
+void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positionX,
+                   const double* __restrict__ positionY,
+                   const double* __restrict__ positionZ,
+                   double* __restrict__ nextPositionX,
+                   double* __restrict__ nextPositionY,
+                   double* __restrict__ nextPositionZ,
+                   double* __restrict__ velocityX,
+                   double* __restrict__ velocityY,
+                   double* __restrict__ velocityZ,
+                   int bodyCount) {
+    __shared__ double tileX[THREADS_PER_BLOCK];
+    __shared__ double tileY[THREADS_PER_BLOCK];
+    __shared__ double tileZ[THREADS_PER_BLOCK];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_TARGET - 1);
+    const int targetInBlock = threadIdx.x / THREADS_PER_TARGET;
+    const int target = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * TARGETS_PER_BLOCK + targetInBlock;
+    const bool active = target < bodyCount;
+
+    const double targetX = active ? positionX[target] : 0.0;
+    const double targetY = active ? positionY[target] : 0.0;
+    const double targetZ = active ? positionZ[target] : 0.0;
+    double forceX = 0.0;
+    double forceY = 0.0;
+    double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+    for (int tileBase = 0; tileBase < bodyCount; tileBase += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {
+        const int source = tileBase + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+        if (source < bodyCount) {
+            tileX[threadIdx.x] = positionX[source];
+            tileY[threadIdx.x] = positionY[source];
+            tileZ[threadIdx.x] = positionZ[source];
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        if (active) {
+            const int tileSize = min(THREADS_PER_BLOCK, bodyCount - tileBase);
+            for (int index = lane; index < tileSize; index += THREADS_PER_TARGET) {
+                const double dx = tileX[index] - targetX;
+                const double dy = tileY[index] - targetY;
+                const double dz = tileZ[index] - targetZ;
+                const double distanceSquared = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double inverseDistance = rsqrt(distanceSquared);
+                const double inverseDistanceCubed =
+                    inverseDistance * inverseDistance * inverseDistance;
+
+                forceX += dx * inverseDistanceCubed;
+                forceY += dy * inverseDistanceCubed;
+                forceZ += dz * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = THREADS_PER_TARGET / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {
+        forceX += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, forceX, offset, THREADS_PER_TARGET);
+        forceY += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, forceY, offset, THREADS_PER_TARGET);
+        forceZ += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, forceZ, offset, THREADS_PER_TARGET);
+    }
+
+    if (lane == 0 && active) {
+        const double newVelocityX = velocityX[target] + DT * forceX;
+        const double newVelocityY = velocityY[target] + DT * forceY;
+        const double newVelocityZ = velocityZ[target] + DT * forceZ;
+
+        velocityX[target] = newVelocityX;
+        velocityY[target] = newVelocityY;
+        velocityZ[target] = newVelocityZ;
+        nextPositionX[target] = targetX + newVelocityX * DT;
+        nextPositionY[target] = targetY + newVelocityY * DT;
+        nextPositionZ[target] = targetZ + newVelocityZ * DT;
+    }
 }

+class DeviceSimulation {
+  public:
+    explicit DeviceSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies)
+        : bodyCount_(static_cast<int>(bodies.size())) {
+        if (bodyCount_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const size_t count = bodies.size();
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&state_), 9 * count * sizeof(double)));
+        hostState_.resize(6 * count);
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+            hostState_[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            hostState_[count + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            hostState_[2 * count + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            hostState_[3 * count + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            hostState_[4 * count + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            hostState_[5 * count + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(position(0, 0), hostState_.data(), 3 * count * sizeof(double),
+                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(velocity(0), hostState_.data() + 3 * count,
+                              3 * count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+
+        // Host staging is only needed again when results are requested.
+        hostState_.clear();
+        hostState_.shrink_to_fit();
+    }
+
+    DeviceSimulation(const DeviceSimulation&) = delete;
+    DeviceSimulation& operator=(const DeviceSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~DeviceSimulation() {
+        if (state_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(state_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    float run(int stepCount) {

+        cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;

+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start));
+
+        if (bodyCount_ > 0) {
+            const int blockCount = (bodyCount_ + TARGETS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / TARGETS_PER_BLOCK;
+            for (int step = 0; step < stepCount; ++step) {
+                advanceBodies<<<blockCount, THREADS_PER_BLOCK>>>(
+                    position(activePosition_, 0), position(activePosition_, 1),
+                    position(activePosition_, 2), position(activePosition_ ^ 1, 0),
+                    position(activePosition_ ^ 1, 1), position(activePosition_ ^ 1, 2),
+                    velocity(0), velocity(1), velocity(2), bodyCount_);
+                activePosition_ ^= 1;
+            }
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop));
+        float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start, stop));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(start));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stop));
+        return elapsedMilliseconds;
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
+        if (bodyCount_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const size_t count = bodies.size();
+        hostState_.resize(6 * count);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostState_.data(), position(activePosition_, 0),
+                              3 * count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostState_.data() + 3 * count, velocity(0),
+                              3 * count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos =
+                Vec3(hostState_[i], hostState_[count + i], hostState_[2 * count + i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(hostState_[3 * count + i], hostState_[4 * count + i],
+                                 hostState_[5 * count + i]);
+        }
+    }
+
+  private:
+    double* position(int buffer, int component) const {
+        return state_ + static_cast<size_t>(buffer * 3 + component) * bodyCount_;
+    }
+
+    double* velocity(int component) const {
+        return state_ + static_cast<size_t>(6 + component) * bodyCount_;
     }
-}
 
+    int bodyCount_ = 0;
+    int activePosition_ = 0;
+    double* state_ = nullptr;

+// A sub-warp cooperatively evaluates one target body. Source positions are
+// staged once per block, so global reads are coalesced and shared by all
+// targets in that block.
+// The destination position arrays form the step barrier: a kernel only reads
+// the immutable current arrays and only writes the next arrays.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS_PER_BLOCK, 4)
+void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positionX,
+                   const double* __restrict__ positionY,
+                   const double* __restrict__ positionZ,
+                   double* __restrict__ nextPositionX,
+                   double* __restrict__ nextPositionY,
+                   double* __restrict__ nextPositionZ,
+                   double* __restrict__ velocityX,
+                   double* __restrict__ velocityY,
+                   double* __restrict__ velocityZ,
+                   int bodyCount) {
+    __shared__ double tileX[THREADS_PER_BLOCK];
+    __shared__ double tileY[THREADS_PER_BLOCK];
+    __shared__ double tileZ[THREADS_PER_BLOCK];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_TARGET - 1);
+    const int targetInBlock = threadIdx.x / THREADS_PER_TARGET;
+    const int target = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * TARGETS_PER_BLOCK + targetInBlock;
+    const bool active = target < bodyCount;
+
+    const double targetX = active ? positionX[target] : 0.0;
+    const double targetY = active ? positionY[target] : 0.0;
+    const double targetZ = active ? positionZ[target] : 0.0;
+    double forceX = 0.0;
+    double forceY = 0.0;
+    double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+    for (int tileBase = 0; tileBase < bodyCount; tileBase += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {
+        const int source = tileBase + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+        if (source < bodyCount) {
+            tileX[threadIdx.x] = positionX[source];
+            tileY[threadIdx.x] = positionY[source];
+            tileZ[threadIdx.x] = positionZ[source];
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        if (active) {
+            const int tileSize = min(THREADS_PER_BLOCK, bodyCount - tileBase);
+            for (int index = lane; index < tileSize; index += THREADS_PER_TARGET) {
+                const double dx = tileX[index] - targetX;
+                const double dy = tileY[index] - targetY;
+                const double dz = tileZ[index] - targetZ;
+                const double distanceSquared = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double inverseDistance = rsqrt(distanceSquared);
+                const double inverseDistanceCubed =
+                    inverseDistance * inverseDistance * inverseDistance;
+
+                forceX += dx * inverseDistanceCubed;
+                forceY += dy * inverseDistanceCubed;
+                forceZ += dz * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = THREADS_PER_TARGET / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {
+        forceX += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, forceX, offset, THREADS_PER_TARGET);
+        forceY += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, forceY, offset, THREADS_PER_TARGET);
+        forceZ += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, forceZ, offset, THREADS_PER_TARGET);
+    }
+
+    if (lane == 0 && active) {
+        const double newVelocityX = velocityX[target] + DT * forceX;
+        const double newVelocityY = velocityY[target] + DT * forceY;
+        const double newVelocityZ = velocityZ[target] + DT * forceZ;
+
+        velocityX[target] = newVelocityX;
+        velocityY[target] = newVelocityY;
+        velocityZ[target] = newVelocityZ;
+        nextPositionX[target] = targetX + newVelocityX * DT;
+        nextPositionY[target] = targetY + newVelocityY * DT;
+        nextPositionZ[target] = targetZ + newVelocityZ * DT;
+    }
 }

+class DeviceSimulation {
+  public:
+    explicit DeviceSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies)
+        : bodyCount_(static_cast<int>(bodies.size())) {
+        if (bodyCount_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const size_t count = bodies.size();
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&state_), 9 * count * sizeof(double)));
+        hostState_.resize(6 * count);
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+            hostState_[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            hostState_[count + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            hostState_[2 * count + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            hostState_[3 * count + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            hostState_[4 * count + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            hostState_[5 * count + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(position(0, 0), hostState_.data(), 3 * count * sizeof(double),
+                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(velocity(0), hostState_.data() + 3 * count,
+                              3 * count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+
+        // Host staging is only needed again when results are requested.
+        hostState_.clear();
+        hostState_.shrink_to_fit();
+    }
+
+    DeviceSimulation(const DeviceSimulation&) = delete;
+    DeviceSimulation& operator=(const DeviceSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~DeviceSimulation() {
+        if (state_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(state_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    float run(int stepCount) {
+        cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
+        cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start));
+
+        if (bodyCount_ > 0) {
+            const int blockCount = (bodyCount_ + TARGETS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / TARGETS_PER_BLOCK;
+            for (int step = 0; step < stepCount; ++step) {
+                advanceBodies<<<blockCount, THREADS_PER_BLOCK>>>(
+                    position(activePosition_, 0), position(activePosition_, 1),
+                    position(activePosition_, 2), position(activePosition_ ^ 1, 0),
+                    position(activePosition_ ^ 1, 1), position(activePosition_ ^ 1, 2),
+                    velocity(0), velocity(1), velocity(2), bodyCount_);
+                activePosition_ ^= 1;
+            }
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop));
+        float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start, stop));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(start));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stop));
+        return elapsedMilliseconds;
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
+        if (bodyCount_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const size_t count = bodies.size();
+        hostState_.resize(6 * count);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostState_.data(), position(activePosition_, 0),
+                              3 * count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostState_.data() + 3 * count, velocity(0),
+                              3 * count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos =
+                Vec3(hostState_[i], hostState_[count + i], hostState_[2 * count + i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(hostState_[3 * count + i], hostState_[4 * count + i],
+                                 hostState_[5 * count + i]);
+        }
+    }
+
+  private:
+    double* position(int buffer, int component) const {
+        return state_ + static_cast<size_t>(buffer * 3 + component) * bodyCount_;
+    }
+
+    double* velocity(int component) const {
+        return state_ + static_cast<size_t>(6 + component) * bodyCount_;
     }
-}

+    int activePosition_ = 0;
+    double* state_ = nullptr;

+// A sub-warp cooperatively evaluates one target body. Source positions are
+// staged once per block, so global reads are coalesced and shared by all
+// targets in that block.
+// The destination position arrays form the step barrier: a kernel only reads
+// the immutable current arrays and only writes the next arrays.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS_PER_BLOCK, 4)
+void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positionX,
+                   const double* __restrict__ positionY,
+                   const double* __restrict__ positionZ,
+                   double* __restrict__ nextPositionX,
+                   double* __restrict__ nextPositionY,
+                   double* __restrict__ nextPositionZ,
+                   double* __restrict__ velocityX,
+                   double* __restrict__ velocityY,
+                   double* __restrict__ velocityZ,
+                   int bodyCount) {
+    __shared__ double tileX[THREADS_PER_BLOCK];
+    __shared__ double tileY[THREADS_PER_BLOCK];
+    __shared__ double tileZ[THREADS_PER_BLOCK];
+
+    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_TARGET - 1);
+    const int targetInBlock = threadIdx.x / THREADS_PER_TARGET;
+    const int target = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * TARGETS_PER_BLOCK + targetInBlock;
+    const bool active = target < bodyCount;
+
+    const double targetX = active ? positionX[target] : 0.0;
+    const double targetY = active ? positionY[target] : 0.0;
+    const double targetZ = active ? positionZ[target] : 0.0;
+    double forceX = 0.0;
+    double forceY = 0.0;
+    double forceZ = 0.0;
+
+    for (int tileBase = 0; tileBase < bodyCount; tileBase += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {
+        const int source = tileBase + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
+        if (source < bodyCount) {
+            tileX[threadIdx.x] = positionX[source];
+            tileY[threadIdx.x] = positionY[source];
+            tileZ[threadIdx.x] = positionZ[source];
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        if (active) {
+            const int tileSize = min(THREADS_PER_BLOCK, bodyCount - tileBase);
+            for (int index = lane; index < tileSize; index += THREADS_PER_TARGET) {
+                const double dx = tileX[index] - targetX;
+                const double dy = tileY[index] - targetY;
+                const double dz = tileZ[index] - targetZ;
+                const double distanceSquared = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double inverseDistance = rsqrt(distanceSquared);
+                const double inverseDistanceCubed =
+                    inverseDistance * inverseDistance * inverseDistance;
+
+                forceX += dx * inverseDistanceCubed;
+                forceY += dy * inverseDistanceCubed;
+                forceZ += dz * inverseDistanceCubed;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
     }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (int offset = THREADS_PER_TARGET / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {
+        forceX += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, forceX, offset, THREADS_PER_TARGET);
+        forceY += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, forceY, offset, THREADS_PER_TARGET);
+        forceZ += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, forceZ, offset, THREADS_PER_TARGET);
+    }
+
+    if (lane == 0 && active) {
+        const double newVelocityX = velocityX[target] + DT * forceX;
+        const double newVelocityY = velocityY[target] + DT * forceY;
+        const double newVelocityZ = velocityZ[target] + DT * forceZ;
+
+        velocityX[target] = newVelocityX;
+        velocityY[target] = newVelocityY;
+        velocityZ[target] = newVelocityZ;
+        nextPositionX[target] = targetX + newVelocityX * DT;
+        nextPositionY[target] = targetY + newVelocityY * DT;
+        nextPositionZ[target] = targetZ + newVelocityZ * DT;
+    }
 }

+class DeviceSimulation {
+  public:
+    explicit DeviceSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies)
+        : bodyCount_(static_cast<int>(bodies.size())) {
+        if (bodyCount_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const size_t count = bodies.size();
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&state_), 9 * count * sizeof(double)));
+        hostState_.resize(6 * count);
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+            hostState_[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
+            hostState_[count + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
+            hostState_[2 * count + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
+            hostState_[3 * count + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
+            hostState_[4 * count + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
+            hostState_[5 * count + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(position(0, 0), hostState_.data(), 3 * count * sizeof(double),
+                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(velocity(0), hostState_.data() + 3 * count,
+                              3 * count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+
+        // Host staging is only needed again when results are requested.
+        hostState_.clear();
+        hostState_.shrink_to_fit();
+    }
+
+    DeviceSimulation(const DeviceSimulation&) = delete;
+    DeviceSimulation& operator=(const DeviceSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~DeviceSimulation() {
+        if (state_ != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(state_);
+        }
+    }
+
+    float run(int stepCount) {
+        cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
+        cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start));
+
+        if (bodyCount_ > 0) {
+            const int blockCount = (bodyCount_ + TARGETS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / TARGETS_PER_BLOCK;
+            for (int step = 0; step < stepCount; ++step) {
+                advanceBodies<<<blockCount, THREADS_PER_BLOCK>>>(
+                    position(activePosition_, 0), position(activePosition_, 1),
+                    position(activePosition_, 2), position(activePosition_ ^ 1, 0),
+                    position(activePosition_ ^ 1, 1), position(activePosition_ ^ 1, 2),
+                    velocity(0), velocity(1), velocity(2), bodyCount_);
+                activePosition_ ^= 1;
+            }
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop));
+        float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start, stop));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(start));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stop));
+        return elapsedMilliseconds;
+    }
+
+    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
+        if (bodyCount_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const size_t count = bodies.size();
+        hostState_.resize(6 * count);
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostState_.data(), position(activePosition_, 0),
+                              3 * count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostState_.data() + 3 * count, velocity(0),
+                              3 * count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
+            bodies[i].pos =
+                Vec3(hostState_[i], hostState_[count + i], hostState_[2 * count + i]);
+            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(hostState_[3 * count + i], hostState_[4 * count + i],
+                                 hostState_[5 * count + i]);
+        }
+    }
+
+  private:
+    double* position(int buffer, int component) const {
+        return state_ + static_cast<size_t>(buffer * 3 + component) * bodyCount_;
+    }
+
+    double* velocity(int component) const {
+        return state_ + static_cast<size_t>(6 + component) * bodyCount_;
     }
-}
 
+    int bodyCount_ = 0;
+    int activePosition_ = 0;
+    double* state_ = nullptr;
+    std::vector<double> hostState_;
+};

    14	constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
    15	constexpr double DT = 0.01;
    16	constexpr int THREADS_PER_BLOCK = 256;
    17	constexpr int THREADS_PER_TARGET = 32;
    18	constexpr int TARGETS_PER_BLOCK = THREADS_PER_BLOCK / THREADS_PER_TARGET;
    19	
    20	void checkCuda(cudaError_t error, const char* operation, const char* file, int line) {
    21	    if (error != cudaSuccess) {
    22	        std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while executing %s: %s\n",
    23	                     file, line, operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));
    24	        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
    25	    }

    31	    double x, y, z;
    32	    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept
    33	        : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}

    37	    Vec3 pos;
    38	    Vec3 vel;

    41	void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    42	    for (auto& body : bodies) {
    43	        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 1.0;
    44	        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 1.0;
    45	        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 1.0;
    46	        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 1.0;
    47	        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 1.0;
    48	        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX)) - 1.0;
    49	    }
    50	}
    51	
    52	// A sub-warp cooperatively evaluates one target body. Source positions are
    53	// staged once per block, so global reads are coalesced and shared by all
    54	// targets in that block.
    55	// The destination position arrays form the step barrier: a kernel only reads
    56	// the immutable current arrays and only writes the next arrays.
    57	__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS_PER_BLOCK, 4)
    58	void advanceBodies(const double* __restrict__ positionX,
    59	                   const double* __restrict__ positionY,
    60	                   const double* __restrict__ positionZ,
    61	                   double* __restrict__ nextPositionX,
    62	                   double* __restrict__ nextPositionY,
    63	                   double* __restrict__ nextPositionZ,
    64	                   double* __restrict__ velocityX,
    65	                   double* __restrict__ velocityY,
    66	                   double* __restrict__ velocityZ,
    67	                   int bodyCount) {
    68	    __shared__ double tileX[THREADS_PER_BLOCK];
    69	    __shared__ double tileY[THREADS_PER_BLOCK];
    70	    __shared__ double tileZ[THREADS_PER_BLOCK];
    71	
    72	    const int lane = threadIdx.x & (THREADS_PER_TARGET - 1);
    73	    const int targetInBlock = threadIdx.x / THREADS_PER_TARGET;
    74	    const int target = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x) * TARGETS_PER_BLOCK + targetInBlock;
    75	    const bool active = target < bodyCount;
    76	
    77	    const double targetX = active ? positionX[target] : 0.0;
    78	    const double targetY = active ? positionY[target] : 0.0;
    79	    const double targetZ = active ? positionZ[target] : 0.0;
    80	    double forceX = 0.0;
    81	    double forceY = 0.0;
    82	    double forceZ = 0.0;
    83	
    84	    for (int tileBase = 0; tileBase < bodyCount; tileBase += THREADS_PER_BLOCK) {
    85	        const int source = tileBase + static_cast<int>(threadIdx.x);
    86	        if (source < bodyCount) {
    87	            tileX[threadIdx.x] = positionX[source];
    88	            tileY[threadIdx.x] = positionY[source];
    89	            tileZ[threadIdx.x] = positionZ[source];
    90	        }
    91	        __syncthreads();
    92	
    93	        if (active) {
    94	            const int tileSize = min(THREADS_PER_BLOCK, bodyCount - tileBase);
    95	            for (int index = lane; index < tileSize; index += THREADS_PER_TARGET) {
    96	                const double dx = tileX[index] - targetX;
    97	                const double dy = tileY[index] - targetY;
    98	                const double dz = tileZ[index] - targetZ;
    99	                const double distanceSquared = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
   100	                const double inverseDistance = rsqrt(distanceSquared);
   101	                const double inverseDistanceCubed =
   102	                    inverseDistance * inverseDistance * inverseDistance;
   103	
   104	                forceX += dx * inverseDistanceCubed;
   105	                forceY += dy * inverseDistanceCubed;
   106	                forceZ += dz * inverseDistanceCubed;
   107	            }
   108	        }
   109	        __syncthreads();
   110	    }
   111	
   112	#pragma unroll
   113	    for (int offset = THREADS_PER_TARGET / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {
   114	        forceX += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, forceX, offset, THREADS_PER_TARGET);
   115	        forceY += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, forceY, offset, THREADS_PER_TARGET);
   116	        forceZ += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, forceZ, offset, THREADS_PER_TARGET);
   117	    }
   118	
   119	    if (lane == 0 && active) {
   120	        const double newVelocityX = velocityX[target] + DT * forceX;
   121	        const double newVelocityY = velocityY[target] + DT * forceY;
   122	        const double newVelocityZ = velocityZ[target] + DT * forceZ;
   123	
   124	        velocityX[target] = newVelocityX;
   125	        velocityY[target] = newVelocityY;
   126	        velocityZ[target] = newVelocityZ;
   127	        nextPositionX[target] = targetX + newVelocityX * DT;
   128	        nextPositionY[target] = targetY + newVelocityY * DT;
   129	        nextPositionZ[target] = targetZ + newVelocityZ * DT;
   130	    }
   131	}
   132	
   133	class DeviceSimulation {
   134	  public:
   135	    explicit DeviceSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies)
   136	        : bodyCount_(static_cast<int>(bodies.size())) {
   137	        if (bodyCount_ == 0) {
   138	            return;
   139	        }
   140	
   141	        const size_t count = bodies.size();
   142	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&state_), 9 * count * sizeof(double)));
   143	        hostState_.resize(6 * count);
   144	
   145	        for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
   146	            hostState_[i] = bodies[i].pos.x;
   147	            hostState_[count + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;
   148	            hostState_[2 * count + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;
   149	            hostState_[3 * count + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;
   150	            hostState_[4 * count + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;
   151	            hostState_[5 * count + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;
   152	        }
   153	
   154	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(position(0, 0), hostState_.data(), 3 * count * sizeof(double),
   155	                              cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   156	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(velocity(0), hostState_.data() + 3 * count,
   157	                              3 * count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   158	
   159	        // Host staging is only needed again when results are requested.
   160	        hostState_.clear();
   161	        hostState_.shrink_to_fit();
   162	    }
   163	
   164	    DeviceSimulation(const DeviceSimulation&) = delete;
   165	    DeviceSimulation& operator=(const DeviceSimulation&) = delete;
   166	
   167	    ~DeviceSimulation() {
   168	        if (state_ != nullptr) {
   169	            cudaFree(state_);
   170	        }
   171	    }
   172	
   173	    float run(int stepCount) {
   174	        cudaEvent_t start = nullptr;
   175	        cudaEvent_t stop = nullptr;
   176	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&start));
   177	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventCreate(&stop));
   178	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(start));
   179	
   180	        if (bodyCount_ > 0) {
   181	            const int blockCount = (bodyCount_ + TARGETS_PER_BLOCK - 1) / TARGETS_PER_BLOCK;
   182	            for (int step = 0; step < stepCount; ++step) {
   183	                advanceBodies<<<blockCount, THREADS_PER_BLOCK>>>(
   184	                    position(activePosition_, 0), position(activePosition_, 1),
   185	                    position(activePosition_, 2), position(activePosition_ ^ 1, 0),
   186	                    position(activePosition_ ^ 1, 1), position(activePosition_ ^ 1, 2),
   187	                    velocity(0), velocity(1), velocity(2), bodyCount_);
   188	                activePosition_ ^= 1;
   189	            }
   190	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   191	        }
   192	
   193	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventRecord(stop));
   194	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventSynchronize(stop));
   195	        float elapsedMilliseconds = 0.0F;
   196	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsedMilliseconds, start, stop));
   197	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(start));
   198	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaEventDestroy(stop));
   199	        return elapsedMilliseconds;
   200	    }
   201	
   202	    void download(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
   203	        if (bodyCount_ == 0) {
   204	            return;
   205	        }
   206	
   207	        const size_t count = bodies.size();
   208	        hostState_.resize(6 * count);
   209	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostState_.data(), position(activePosition_, 0),
   210	                              3 * count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
   211	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostState_.data() + 3 * count, velocity(0),
   212	                              3 * count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
   213	
   214	        for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
   215	            bodies[i].pos =
   216	                Vec3(hostState_[i], hostState_[count + i], hostState_[2 * count + i]);
   217	            bodies[i].vel = Vec3(hostState_[3 * count + i], hostState_[4 * count + i],
   218	                                 hostState_[5 * count + i]);
   219	        }
   220	    }
   221	
   222	  private:
   223	    double* position(int buffer, int component) const {
   224	        return state_ + static_cast<size_t>(buffer * 3 + component) * bodyCount_;
   225	    }
   226	
   227	    double* velocity(int component) const {
   228	        return state_ + static_cast<size_t>(6 + component) * bodyCount_;
   229	    }
   230	
   231	    int bodyCount_ = 0;
   232	    int activePosition_ = 0;
   233	    double* state_ = nullptr;
   234	    std::vector<double> hostState_;
   235	};
   236	
   237	double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
   238	    double energy = 0.0;
   239	    const size_t count = bodies.size();
   240	
   241	    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
   242	        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + body.vel.y * body.vel.y +
   243	                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
   244	    }
   245	
   246	    for (size_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
   247	        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < count; ++j) {
   248	            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
   249	            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
   250	            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
   251	            const double distance = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
   252	            energy -= 1.0 / distance;
   253	        }
   254	    }
   255	    return energy;
